### 0.68 Owner instruction — 2026-10-30: complete the CMS-driven website (final master directive)

D-12, D-21, OD-04, OD-100, OD-113 (d), OD-118 (d), OD-120, the tracking, privacy, inventory, redirect and AI-provider decisions and the real-inventory entry gate unchanged. No phase accepted and no phase status changed; OD-139 to OD-141 stay implemented, not accepted.

| ID | Decision |
|---|---|
| OD-143 | **Owner instruction (2026-10-30, pasted "Final master directive"):** complete the public website as a CMS-driven workspace discovery and conversion site (Awfis.com as a functional reference only, nothing copied): header Spaces · Locations · Solutions · Community · About · Resources · Search · Book a Tour with a working search and accessible drop-downs; home page sections; Spaces, Locations (filters), Solutions (the eight segments), Community (events clearly marked as examples, no registration), About, Resources (listing, topics, detail, sample articles identifiable), contact; the CMS as the source of truth, extended where needed, with author ≠ approver kept; a browser demonstration of editing, saving, reloading, previewing, approving and of unpublished content staying private, in an isolated environment; **demo content allowed** with an explicit identification mechanism, never inventing prices, availability, seats, hours, amenities, distances, testimonials, customers, awards, contacts, legal commitments, event dates, registration or booking confirmations; SEO, accessibility and browser checks at desktop, tablet and phone. Never write to, migrate, seed or clear `smart_go`; `smartgro_preview` read-only; tests only on `smartgro_testing`; no request to port 8001; no commit, push, deploy or package change. |
| OD-143 (a) | **Sample-content marker (the identification mechanism).** A page is sample content when its layout is **"Sample page (example content)"** (`template = sample`, `PagePayload::SAMPLE_TEMPLATE`); an article when it carries the topic with the address `sample-content` (`PostTopic::SAMPLE_SLUG`, `Post::isSample()`). Either way the page shows a notice above the header ("Sample content. This page shows example text for SmartGro's team to replace. It is not SmartGro information."), is `noindex, follow`, is left out of the sitemap, gets no Article structured data, and is labelled "Sample content" in the insights lists, the home page and search; the sample topic's own page is `noindex` and left out of the sitemap too. Staff remove the marker by choosing another layout or topic through the normal approval, so nothing is published as SmartGro information by accident. No new column or table. |
| OD-143 (b) | **Built (local, uncommitted).** **Search:** `GET /search` (`SearchController`, `SiteSearch`, 60 a minute per visitor): listed locations, shown workspace types, published pages, articles, consented customer stories and general FAQs through the same scopes as their pages, grouped, 8 a group, substring match with wildcards escaped, at least 2 and at most 100 characters, never indexed; Search icon in the header and the phone menu. **CMS:** a "Cards with links" section (`LinkCardsBlock`, 2–9 cards with optional links) and links to "a part of this website" in every section link (`ContentLink::TO_SECTION`, shown only while that part has something to show); menu links to **a workspace type** (`NavigationPayload::CATEGORY`, stored as a link to the product, shown only while the type has a page; no schema change) for the Spaces drop-down; a drop-down with more than six links opens as a two-column panel, under its own button where the browser supports anchor positioning, and its button reports `aria-expanded`. **Home page:** "Who we work with" (the offered segments from records, each linking only to its published solutions page), "Latest insights" (three newest published articles) and "Questions" (the first four general FAQs), each only while it has something to show. **Insights:** sample badge on listing and topic cards. Reserved address `search`. |
| OD-143 (c) | **Demo content (demo and test databases only).** `docs/operations/scripts/browser/seed-sample-content.php` (refuses any database but `smartgro_demo` and `smartgro_testing`) publishes through the real workflow (author, a different approver, the publisher), all with the sample layout or topic: the eight solutions pages `solutions/{segment}` from `customer_segments` (needs, workspace types to consider with the catalogue's own one-line descriptions, how it works, questions, enquiry and tour links) and their overview; Community with two **example events** (no date, location or registration: "Registration: not open"); About (tagline and "Coworking and managed workspaces in Noida, Delhi NCR." only); three example articles; the main menu (Spaces and Locations drop-downs, Solutions, Community, About, Resources drop-down), footer and legal menus. **Events are CMS pages for now (interim):** a dated, registrable event type needs the owner's decision on what events are, who runs them and whether visitors register (and the privacy notice that would need); until then no event shows a date or registration. |
| OD-143 (d) | **CMS demonstration** (`../testing/evidence/phase6/2026-10-30-od143/cms-demonstration/`), demo server 8768 on `smartgro_demo`, About page, Chromium at 1440 px: the author changed the heading, body, a button's label and destination and a card, saved, reloaded (all kept), previewed (signed staff link, all changes, not indexable); the public page and search showed none of it while it was a draft, waiting for approval, or approved but unpublished; the author could not approve their own version ("You worked on this version, so someone else must review it."); a different person approved; the approver could not publish what they approved ("You approved this version, so someone else must publish it."); the author published and the public page showed every change. The preview link without a staff session, or with a changed signature, leads to the staff login. 14 of 14 steps passed on the third run; the sample content was published again afterwards. |
| OD-143 (e) | **Verification (2026-10-30).** Full suite, each part exit code 0: 354 (1,436); 240 (744); 29 (97); Feature/Filament 235 (1,526); Feature/Http, Services and Views 436 (2,107): **1,294 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (5,910 assertions)**. New tests: `SearchControllerTest` (5), sample articles and pages (`PublishedContentControllerTest`, `PageControllerTest`), cards and sample layout (`PagePayloadTest`), home sections (`HomeControllerTest`, 2), workspace-type menu links (`NavigationPayloadTest`, `PublicShellTest`). Browser kit (`../testing/evidence/phase6/2026-10-30-od143/`), each exit code 0: public, enquiry, tour, back office. Demo (8768): home, solutions, a segment, community, an event, about, search, an article and insights at 1440 and 390 px with no overflow, console error or failed request, the notice on every sample page; Spaces, Locations and Resources drop-downs open under their buttons at 1440 and 1024 px, Escape closes them and returns focus, Tab enters the list; the phone menu lists every group's links; city filters change the results (meeting rooms: A-70, H-105; dedicated desks: A-71; private offices: A-70). axe-core (WCAG 2.0–2.2 A and AA) on 11 new or changed pages at 1440 and 390 px: 0 violations, one `h1` each, sample pages `noindex, follow`, search `noindex, nofollow`; the one defect found (the sample topic page indexable and in the sitemap) was fixed and its tests (40, 377 assertions) pass; the full suite above ran before that fix. Screenshots `../design/review/2026-10-30-od143/`. `smart_go` (44 migrations, batch 10, no content change since 27 October) and `smartgro_preview` (68, batch 6) unchanged, checked read-only; no request to port 8001; nothing committed, pushed or deployed; no package changed. |

The demo now runs as its own user service (`systemctl --user status smartgro-demo-8768`). ### 0.69 Owner instruction — 2026-10-30: a livelier, fuller, world-class website

OD-143's safeguards and the decisions it lists unchanged. No phase accepted and no phase status changed.

| ID | Decision |
|---|---|
| OD-144 | **Owner instruction (2026-10-30):** "I want a world class platform… limited pages and content… search across the globe and bring as many features… animation is missing, liveliness is missing… make the home page attractive and beautiful, streamline the details and sections." Read as: survey leading coworking websites worldwide, adopt what can be built without invented business facts, add motion and more pages and content, and tidy the home page. |
| OD-144 (a) | **Survey** (14 operators: WeWork, WeWork India, Regus, Industrious, TOG/Fora, Mindspace, Convene, Servcorp, Awfis, Smartworks, 91Springboard, Innov8, IndiQube, Second Home; marketplaces Upflex and CoworkingCafe; patterns only, nothing copied). Built from it now: scroll reveals, staggered entrances and slow photo zoom on hover; a ticker; counters; careers, partner (brokers and landlords) and accessibility pages; a richer insights library with search; a filter on all locations. **Needs SmartGro's facts first** (not built, no placeholders): video or photo hero (D-12), amenities per location and their filter, walking times to the metro, Google rating, client logos and testimonials, prices or "from" prices, a dated counter of members, events with dates and registration, 360° tours (OD-118 d), a map (needs coordinates, and no external map service, OD-107), meeting-room or day-pass booking by time (needs real availability), and a fraud warning about payments (common on Indian sites; SmartGro's own payment facts). |
| OD-144 (b) | **Motion (local, uncommitted), all CSS except a 0.4 kB counter script, none under reduced motion:** sections and cards rise into view as they are scrolled to (`[data-reveal]`, `[data-reveal-group]`, CSS scroll-driven animations; browsers without them show the page as it is); hero text rises in turn, its image fades in, a soft light drifts behind it and the locations card floats; a ticker of the workspace types and locations on the website (decorative, `aria-hidden`, stops under the pointer); the location and workspace-type counts count up from records; the header gains a shadow once the page scrolls; pages cross-fade (view transitions); cards lift and their images zoom on hover; CMS heroes and the cards, features, stats and call-to-action sections reveal too. The workspace finder is not revealed, so it is fully visible at the fold. |

Safeguards kept: no sample content in production; `BACKOFFICE_SAMPLE_CONTENT` not enabled there; never `access:sync` on `smart_go`; preview read-only; no requests to port 8001; no commit or push. **Interpretation recorded:** no deployed production environment exists (D-14 open, OD-79). The database the authorisation refers to is **`smart_go`**: the only database with the 25 pending migrations the OD-149 report named, the `.env` default (`APP_ENV=local`) and the owner's own server's database. |

No price, capacity, facility, service scope, availability, address or guarantee is written. The run is repeatable: the first run on each server added 14 versions (13 pages and the main menu), the second added none. **CMS (code, tests):** page buttons and cards can link to **a workspace type**, shown only while the type has its page (a published offering), like a link to a part of the website (`ContentLink`, `WorkspaceCatalogue::categoryPath`). The page pickers in the menu editor and in page sections mark sample pages "· sample content". The menu editor's help says descriptions and icons show in a drop-down with categories. **Fix found during the database work:** on a database without insight topics, `/sitemap.xml` failed (500), selecting `posts.topic_ids` without the OD-120 guard; it is now guarded, and the OD-120 test covers the sitemap. |

On the demo and the site: **Dedicated Desks → `/workspaces/dedicated-desks`**, **Flexible Desks → `/workspaces/hot-desks`** (the type is named "Hot desks": "Flexible seating: take any open desk when you need one"; the menu label is the owner's), **Private Offices → `/workspaces/private-cabins`** ("Private offices and cabins"). **Managed Workspaces → its sample page `/workspace-solutions/managed-workspaces`**, because managed offices have no published offering, so no type page exists (404). The three mapped sample pages stay published and editable (noindex, out of the sitemap). They are not in the menu. Their main button "See locations" goes to the type page, and related cards elsewhere link to the type page, so no offering has two competing destinations. The mapping follows the records: when a type gains or loses its page, a rerun of the script moves the link. Business Solutions stay on their sample pages (decision 2 covers the four Workspaces offerings). Where a matching type exists (Meeting Rooms ↔ meeting rooms, Training Rooms ↔ conference and training rooms, Virtual Office ↔ virtual office), the sample page's main button links to the type page while it exists (today only meeting rooms). |---|---|
| OD-151 | **Owner instruction (2026-10-31, pasted "Continue the SmartGro project from OD-150 … evidence-based verification").** An audit of OD-150 with read-only checks on `smart_go`, no database changes, no `access:sync`, no seeding or publication, the preview read-only, and nothing sent to port 8001. Recorded in **`../testing/OD151_VERIFICATION_REPORT_2026-10-31.md`**; evidence in `../testing/evidence/phase14/2026-10-31-od151/`. The owner clarified that `smart_go` is the database of their own server, **not a deployed production environment**, which has not been established. **Verified independently:** live `smart_go` equals OD-150's recorded after-state; the backup's SHA-256 matches, and the backup was **restored again** (into the emptied `smartgro_testing`) and equals the recorded pre-update state; backup plus the 25 migration files reproduce live `smart_go`'s schema; `migrate:rollback --batch=11` returns the copy exactly to the backup state. **Also verified:** sitemaps on 8080 and 8768 (200, `application/xml`, well-formed, only published addresses answering 200, no sample page or article); the 13 offerings, their destinations, notices, noindex and sitemap exclusion (browser checks, 0 failures); editor fields (read-only render); approval separation (policy and tests). 266 relevant tests passed. |
| OD-151 (a) | **Corrections to OD-150's records (OD-150's rows and report left unchanged):** (1) The update added **six** nullable columns to four existing tables (`faqs.property_id`, `faqs.product_id`, `navigation_items.description`, `navigation_items.icon`, `posts.topic_ids`, `products.allowed_inventory_modes`), not "4", and **43** foreign keys. (2) The rollback commands need credentials (`-u root -p`), and `migrate:rollback --batch=11` is more precise than `--step=25`. (3) Between reruns of the sample script, a Workspaces menu link whose type loses its page goes to the enquiry form ("Ask us") or hides, as any workspace-type link does (OD-145), not to the sample page. (4) On `smart_go` the sample-content switch evaluates to **allowed** (`APP_ENV=local`). Publication there is prevented today only by having one active staff account (A-24). |
| OD-151 (b) | **Findings for the next stage (none fixed in this audit):** (1) **23 of the 25 migrations applied to `smart_go` are untracked files**, only in this working tree (A-26). (2) The backups are single copies on this machine (A-27). (3) The sitemap lists `/insights` and topic pages whose articles are all samples (`Sitemap::insights()` counts sample articles), so a minimal code fix is proposed. (4) Spaces readiness, computed read-only: `access:sync` would add 14 permissions and 46 role links, remove none, write 6 audit entries and clear the permission cache; `db:seed --class=ReferenceDataSeeder` would add only the 6 space types (and 6 audit entries); both are idempotent; a plain `db:seed` would also run `access:sync` and create a factory "Test User", so it must never run on `smart_go`. |

### 0.86 Owner report — 2026-11-05: the default seed fails ("There is no permission named `allocations.manage`")

No phase status or acceptance changed; nothing committed, pushed or deployed; no package changed.

| ID | Decision |
|---|---|
| OD-166 | **Owner's words (2026-11-05): "also all default seed i come with this error"**, with Spatie's `PermissionDoesNotExist` for `allocations.manage` from `SyncAccessControl`. **Found (binary log):** after OD-165 (12:18) the owner ran `migrate:refresh` twice (12:27:15 and 12:27:41; each rolled every migration back and stopped after three, because the audit-log migration has no `down()`), then `migrate:fresh --seed` (12:30:43): every table dropped, 73 migrations, and the default seed failed and rolled back, leaving `smart_go` empty (Owner sign-in, restored data and sample content gone). **Cause:** `DatabaseSeeder` used `WithoutModelEvents`; Spatie refreshes its cached permission list on a model event, so on an empty database the list loaded before the capabilities were created stayed empty, and the first grant (`allocations.manage`, first alphabetically) failed. On a database with permissions the grant is never reached, which is why `db:seed` at 12:01 worked. Reproduced with `migrate:fresh --seed` on `smartgro_testing`. |
| OD-166 (a) | **Owner's answers (AskUserQuestion):** default seed → **"Everything + dummy admin (Recommended)"**; `smart_go` → **"Restore 12:17 backup + samples (Recommended)"**. |
| OD-166 (b) | **Fixed (uncommitted):** `SyncAccessControl` forgets the cached permissions after creating them, before granting (works with events off); `DatabaseSeeder` no longer uses `WithoutModelEvents` or creates the factory "Test User", and runs `access:sync`, `ReferenceDataSeeder` and, outside production, `SampleContentSeeder` (which creates the dummy staff when missing); `DummyStaffSeeder` adds a dummy Super Admin (`admin@dummy.test`). Tests: a new `SyncAccessControlTest` case failed with the owner's exact error before the fix and passes after; `DatabaseSeederTest` (empty database to everything, twice; production only roles and reference data); `tests/Feature/Database` and `SyncAccessControlTest` 24 passed; `migrate:fresh --seed` on `smartgro_testing` with the database cache store: exit 0. Pint passed. The reference seeder's A-28 combinations come with the default seed (A-28 still open). |
| OD-166 (c) | **`smart_go` restored:** the empty state saved (`~/smartgro-backups/smart_go-2026-11-05-after-owner-migrate-fresh-1230.sql`); the 12:17 backup (checksum verified) restored, equal to the 12:17 row counts in all 68 tables; rehearsed on a copy, then `SampleContentSeeder` on `smart_go`, identical to the rehearsal: the Owner (user 4) back, 7 dummy accounts (new shared password, shown to the owner once), 29 sample pages, 10 articles, 5 FAQs, main and footer menus, 4 profile drafts. Read-only smoke as the Owner: public 50 × 200, back office 38 × 200. The owner's server stayed running (requests during the few seconds of reloading may have failed). The 12:01 "Test User" is in the 12:17 backup and so still exists (no role). Evidence: `../testing/evidence/phase14/2026-11-05-od166-default-seed/`. |

### 0.93 Owner instruction — 2026-11-10: Phase 9 P9-D, pipeline and deals

No commit, push or deployment; `smart_go` and `smartgro_preview` not accessed; the three new migrations applied only to `smartgro_testing`; no cache or service changed; no phase status or acceptance changed.

| ID | Decision |
|---|---|
| OD-173 | **Owner's brief (2026-11-10), pasted:** "Continue SmartGro Phase 9, package P9-D: Pipeline and Deals", continuing the partial work of OD-170. Explicit instructions: `app/Models/Lead.php` must remain unchanged (its proposed edit had been declined); keep the three migrations, the models, the enum, the factories and `leads.pipeline.manage`; complete the two policy stubs by the existing conventions; no other role gains a permission; invent no stage names, ordering, outcomes, defaults or business values; existing inventory and booking behaviour unchanged; screens hidden without the tables; tests only on `smartgro_testing`. The pasted brief ended mid-sentence at its testing list ("Existing P9-A and"); the regression runs below cover P9-A to P9-C. The earlier P9-D files (OD-170, 2026-11-06) were kept and continued. |
| OD-173 (a) | **P9-D built (locally, uncommitted, not accepted): pipeline and deals (FR-CRM-4, BR-CRM-7).**<br>**Pipeline stages:** Sales → Pipeline stages, Super Admin only, new capability `leads.pipeline.manage` (SECURITY_MODEL §3.1 "Assignment rules, pipeline stages", OD-36). A stage has a name, position, meaning (open, won or lost; one column instead of the ERD's `is_won`/`is_lost`), optional probability and "offered". **None exists (D-09):** until the Super Admin adds an open stage no deal can be opened, and until a won or lost stage exists none can be won or lost. A stage's meaning is fixed once used; stages are switched off, never deleted.<br>**Deals:** opened from an enquiry's page (Deals panel), one open deal per enquiry and none on a closed enquiry; seats, monthly value and expected close as typed (optional, never filled in); owner (the opener; changed with the enquiry's assignment permission); moves between open stages; won or lost once; history from the audit log. Deals follow the enquiry permissions and scope ("Leads, contacts, deals" is one matrix row; BR-CRM-6): no role gains a new permission except the Super Admin's `leads.pipeline.manage`.<br>**Proposed spaces** from the canonical inventory, at the enquiry's location, in use, shaped as an allocation would be, with today's availability; nothing reserved.<br>**Won deal → allocations:** new inventory action `AllocateWonDeal`: deal locked, then the proposed spaces in ascending id order, each checked again with `SpaceAvailability::refusalForAllocation`; all or none; once only. Existing inventory and booking actions unchanged.<br>`app/Models/Lead.php` unchanged (owner instruction): the enquiry panel builds the relation from the deal side.<br>Tables `pipeline_stages`, `deals`, `deal_space` (ERD C-40): migrations added, applied only to `smartgro_testing`; guarded so the owner's server on `smart_go` hides everything deal-related. **Interpretations, for A-42:** one open deal per enquiry; proposals limited to the enquiry's location; a Sales/CRM Executive's win allocates at once (BR-CRM-7 as written); closed deals final; the enquiry's status unchanged by a deal. |
| OD-173 (b) | **Checks (P9-D).** New tests: `DealPipelineTest` 19, `DealSpacesTest` 16 (with datasets), `WinDealTest` 16, `PipelineStageScreensTest` 9, `DealScreensTest` 11, `DealConcurrencyTest` 3 (two deals winning the same cabin, one deal submitted twice, a won deal against a direct allocation of the last pool seats; each exactly one winner). The concurrency test **found a real defect during the build**: `WinDeal` read the enquiry before locking the spaces, so MySQL's snapshot hid an allocation committed meanwhile and both deals allocated the same cabin. Fixed by taking every lock (deal, proposals, spaces in id order) with locking reads before any plain read; three runs since, all passed. Related set (CRM and inventory actions, Sales screens, access sync, design previews, both inventory concurrency files, scope policies, CRM services, architecture): **373 passed**, exit 0 (`../testing/evidence/phase9/2026-11-10-p9d/pest/related.out`). Mutation checks in an isolated copy, never in the live tree: 9 of 9 decision mutations caught, plus the two table guards removed together (each alone is a deliberate back-up for the other) (`mutations.out`). Test values for stages are set by the tests only. Browser kit `CHECKS=admin` on `smartgro_testing`, with test-only stages and two deals added to the fixtures (`evidence/phase9/2026-11-10-p9d/admin/`): **exit 0**, 114 screen checks, 0 failures, 0 axe violations, 0 overflow; the deal and stage screens 200 for the Super Admin, 403 for the other roles, 404 for a deal outside the Sales user's scope. The first run (`admin-first-run/`) found one axe violation (`scrollable-region-focusable`: the empty deals list scrolled sideways at 360 px with nothing to reach by keyboard); fixed by showing the secondary columns from tablet width (`visibleFrom('md')`) in the deals list and both deal panels. |
| OD-173 (c) | **Incident, corrected:** `vendor/bin/pint --dirty` reformatted two other sessions' untracked evidence scripts (OD-171 `activate-space-types.php`, OD-172 `testing-db-check.php`). Both were restored from their sessions' scratchpad copies (Pint on each copy reproduced the damaged file exactly, proving the copies were the originals); OD-171's recorded SHA-256 verifies again; both sessions were told and confirmed. Pint now runs only on this package's own files. |

No commit, push or deployment; no phase status or acceptance changed; no package changed. `smart_go` written once (content only, no migration), after a backup and a rehearsal, as the owner chose. `smartgro_testing` used in turn with the other session (OD-177).

|---|---|
| OD-176 | **Owner's words (2026-11-10, with a screenshot of `/enquire` on port 8000 showing "Online enquiries open soon" and "Contact details are on their way"):** "create the demo details for every section like this and let it be modifiable from cms", then "alogn with images and all". **Found on `smart_go` (read only):** contact and social settings had no values; no privacy notice (so the enquiry form was closed, OD-111 c) and no tour hours (so the tour form was closed too); the four location profiles were example-text drafts that could not be published, because no workspace type was recorded for any location (BR-PROP-3); no offerings, so no workspace pages; no testimonials or case studies. **Owner's answers (AskUserQuestion):** sections → all four offered ("Contact & social details", "Privacy notice + tour hours", "Locations + workspace listings", "Testimonials & case studies"); images → **"Labelled stock (Recommended)"**; contact details → **"Obvious placeholders (Recommended)"**; where → **"smart_go after rehearsal (Recommended)"**. This sets OD-111 (c) aside on this local database: the forms open on a sample notice until SmartGro publishes its own. |
| OD-176 (a) | **Rule change, as chosen (BR-MED-2):** a location or workspace profile may hold a licensed stock photo **only where sample content is allowed** (`HasWebsiteProfile::SAMPLE_PHOTO_ORIGINS`, checked in `photoRule()` on every save, submit and publish). A stock photo needs no link to the property, because the website never presents it as one: `WorkspaceCatalogue` gives it the label "Stock photo, not a SmartGro location". The label is shown on the location page (main photo, thumbnails as "Stock photo", gallery), the workspace page, the home page's location tiles (new `site/photo-label` component), and the home hero and workspace-group captions (`HomeController` uses the label instead of "SmartGro A-70, …"). Where sample content is not allowed (the owner's server: `BACKOFFICE_SAMPLE_CONTENT=false`), a stock photo is refused with "Stock photos can only be sample content…", so it has to be replaced before the profile changes. The photo picker (`PropertyProfileForm::photoOptions`, used by both profile editors) offers stock photos only while sample content is allowed, and always lists those a profile already holds, marked "stock photo, sample content only". Concept visuals and renders are still refused, as is another property's photography. |
| OD-176 (b) | **Content (`DemoContentSeeder`, rerunnable, refuses production; also called by `DatabaseSeeder` outside production, after the other session's `SpaceListingSeeder`).** Everything goes through the real actions and workflow as the dummy staff: Marketing Manager writes and publishes, the second Marketing Manager approves, the Operations Manager records workspace types (`SaveProperty`), and the Property Manager sets tour hours (`SaveTourRule`). Content someone else published or is working on is left alone. **Contact (placeholders that reach nobody):** phone `+91 00000 00000`, email `hello@smartgro.example` (a reserved domain), WhatsApp `+910000000000`, "Example address for SmartGro to replace, Noida, Uttar Pradesh", "Example: Monday to Saturday, 9:00 to 19:00"; **social:** LinkedIn, Instagram, Facebook, X and YouTube addresses ending `smartgro-example-replace-me` (or similar), which no account uses. **Privacy:** `/legal/privacy` in the sample layout ("Sample content", never indexed), titled "Privacy notice (sample)", opening with "This is a sample privacy notice, not SmartGro's own"; what it says the forms record is what the website records, and the rest is marked example text. The legal menu links to it. **Tour hours:** Monday to Friday 10:00–18:00 and Saturday 10:00–14:00, 30-minute tours, one at a time, at each location (24 rules); a location with tour hours keeps them. **Locations:** all 7 active workspace types offered at all 4 locations (28 offerings; which types each location offers is invented). The 4 profile drafts were carried on and published as "Workspaces at SmartGro {code}, {sector}, Noida", with "Example text for SmartGro to replace…", example getting-there, parking and accessibility lines, and 4 photos each. **Workspaces:** 28 profiles, "{type} at SmartGro {code}", described "Example text for SmartGro to replace…" from the catalogue summary, with an example "Included" list and 2 photos each. **Photos:** 7 new CC0 StockSnap photos in `database/seeders/media/demo/` (re-encoded at 2,400 px, without people, logos or signage; recorded as licensed stock with licence CC0 1.0, the photographer and the StockSnap page), plus the 3 banner stock photos of OD-169. **Customers:** 2 example testimonials, by "Example member (fictional)" at "Example Design Studio (fictional)" and "Example Software Team (fictional)", each opening "Example testimonial:". They appear inside 2 example case studies (`/customers/example-design-studio`, `/customers/example-software-team`), titled "Example: …" with a stock photo. Consent fields hold "EXAMPLE (OD-176): fictional, no real person or company. Replace before launch." (testimonials and case studies have no sample flag, so the label is in their visible text, and the case studies are not marked noindex). |

|---|---|
| OD-177 | **Owner's words (2026-11-10):** "spaces and solution have same sort of listing, let itmake a difference, soltions is okay but space will have the listing of all the nameof the spaces smartgro has in the listing which is A-70, A-71, H-105, A-23, these all , let it be modifed and managed from, cms so that admin can add many more spaces as well, let sub the sub header under spaces will again opn and show all the spaces listed under this, even if not availabe let the admin remove it using toggle button from cms bu on or off , if on, let admin add details, as of now you add as a ademo for all the spaces under all the space type atleast 2-3 demo for all if not available or if available let it use the original apaces". **Owner's answers (AskUserQuestion):** layout → "e.g dedicated desk and then show the space under it at what location", read as a tab per space type with its spaces grouped by location; demo count → **"3 each, 72 total"**; On/Off → **"Off is instant, On needs approval (Recommended)"** (OD-58 holds); `smart_go` → **"Yes, after tests (Recommended)"**. **Found:** `smart_go` had the 6 approved space types (OD-171) and the 4 open properties, but no inventory spaces (0 floors, 0 spaces), so every listing starts as an example. |
| OD-177 (a) | **Built (local, uncommitted).** *Back office:* Website → **Spaces on the website** (`SpaceListing`, table `space_listings`, ERD C-41): location, space type, optional inventory space (must be at the same location, of the same type and not decommissioned; the inventory is never changed), name, address `/spaces/{slug}` (filled in from location and name), summary, details, optional seats, up to 8 features, position, an "Example space" switch (published only where sample content is allowed, OD-149). It is revisioned content like FAQs (draft; someone else approves; a third person may publish), appears in "Waiting for approval", and has a signed preview. The list shows each space's location and type and an **On / Off** switch: Off takes it off the website at once (`UnpublishContent`, reason recorded in the audit log). On sends its last content for approval as a new version and stays off until that version is published; if a version is already in progress, nothing new is created. A space never published is finished in its editor. *Website:* each shown space (published, at a location that is open, or opening with a date) has a page with type, location (linked while the location is listed), summary, details, seats, features, other spaces of the type (same location first), "Ask about this space" (the enquiry form with the location, and the workspace type only when the space type belongs to exactly one) and "Book a tour" while those forms are open, otherwise Contact. Example spaces carry the sample notice and "Example space", and are not indexed. *Menu:* a new main-menu link kind **"A space type"** (`space_type`, allowed only as a link inside a drop-down, and only where the table exists). In the header, a drop-down holding one shows a row of space-type tabs (an even grid of three when there are more than four, with counts), and under the chosen tab its spaces in a column per location, with the area. The group's plain links ("All workspace types") and "Example spaces: not yet confirmed by SmartGro." are at the foot. The tabs reuse the existing flyout script (Up/Down between tabs), so `app.js` is unchanged. On phones each type expands to its spaces, each with its location. A type with no shown space has no tab. Guarded by `SpaceListing::isAvailable()`: without the table, no tab, page or screen and no query. *Content (`SpaceListingSeeder`, rerunnable, refuses production; called by `DatabaseSeeder` outside production):* for each listable location and space type, the inventory's own spaces marked publicly listable and active, or else 3 example spaces (Cabin C1–C3, Desk zone D1–D3, Open zone F1–F3, Meeting room M1–M3, Training room T1 / Conference room T2 / Board room T3, Office suite S1–S3), with example summaries, details and features and no seats. The main menu's Spaces drop-down becomes "All workspace types" plus six tabs (Private cabins, Dedicated desks, Flexible seating, Meeting rooms, Conference rooms, Office suites); its 7 workspace-type links move out, since Solutions and `/workspaces` carry them. A space someone switched off, content someone else published or is working on, and a menu someone else published are left alone. This replaces OD-174's Spaces content; OD-174's fallback still serves workspace-type links elsewhere. |
| OD-177 (b) | **Checks.** New tests: `SpaceListingTest` 8 (page, form links, 404s, example labels, related spaces, the tabs by location on wide screens and phones, menu rules and round trip, nothing without the table), `SpaceListingScreensTest` 6 (list and refusal, a real create through the form, inventory and example rules, Off at once with the audit reason, On through approval and "already in progress", preview), `SpaceListingSeederTest` 4 (72 through the workflow, the menu, no change on rerun; inventory spaces instead of examples; a switched-off space and the team's menu left alone; production refused). `DatabaseSeederTest` expects 72. Change: +1 table, migrations 75 → 76 (batch 13), space_listings +72, revisions +73 (72 spaces and 1 menu version), navigation_items 65 → 64, activity_log +292, the main menu updated in place. **On `smart_go`** (unchanged since the backup; the owner's servers running; array cache and session): the same two commands, 21:41:03–21:41:15 IST. The change was identical to the rehearsal. **Read-only smoke on `smart_go`:** six tabs of 12 spaces, the example note, space pages 200, an unknown space 404, public 11 × 200 + 1 × 302, back office 45 × 200 (the three new screens included) + 6 × 302, no error. The only log line is the 404 monitor's refused write for the test's unknown address, which shows the session was read only. The copy was dropped and the temporary credentials file deleted. No other SmartGro session was active, so nothing had to be coordinated. **Built (local, uncommitted).** *Photo:* a space may have one photo from the media library: `space_listings.media_asset_id` (ERD C-42), guarded by `SpaceListing::hasPhotos()`, so without the column there is no field and no query. The rule is the same as for a location's photos (`HasWebsiteProfile::photoRule()`, OD-176): photography of the space's own location (SmartGro or commissioned) with a valid licence, or, only where sample content is allowed, a licensed stock photo. The page shows the photo beside the heading, as on a workspace page, with its credit; a stock photo carries "Stock photo, not a SmartGro location" (`site/photo-label`). The photo is hidden once its licence expires or its file is missing. `MediaUsage` counts a shown space's photo as public use, so its renditions are public only while the space is shown, and counts any listing or version using it as a reference, so the library will not delete it. The preview shows a version's photo. *Content:* `SpaceListingSeeder` gives each example space a CC0 stock photo already in the library, chosen by space type from OD-176's demo photos and OD-169's banner photos (`EXAMPLE_PHOTOS`; a missing photo is left out). `DatabaseSeeder` now runs it after `DemoContentSeeder`, whose photos it uses. *Search:* shown spaces that are not examples are in the sitemap and in Website → Search titles (type "Space", title "{name} at SmartGro {code}", the summary as description, a link to the editor). Example spaces stay out, because they are marked noindex. *Keyboard:* in `resources/js/app.js` the drop-down categories and tabs also move with Left and Right. **Checks.** New tests: `SpaceListingTest` +3 (photo, stock label, rendition 200 while shown and 404 once off; sitemap and search titles with examples left out; no `media_asset_id` query or field without the column), `SpaceListingScreensTest` +1 (own location's photography accepted; another location's refused; stock refused without sample content and accepted with it), `SpaceListingSeederTest` +1 (photos from the library, label on the page). The full suite was not run. |

## 2. Risks

| ID | Risk | Classification | State / mitigation evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-1 | An existing system must stay authoritative, changing the inventory model | **Resolved** (OD-06: no existing software) | SmartGro is the master. `external_*` columns kept for future integrations. |
| R-2 | No version control | **Resolved** | Git initialised, with a reviewed baseline commit (§3). Residual: no off-machine copy yet (R-11). |
| R-3 | No vector logo or real photography | **Requires client input** (D-11b, D-12) | The raster logo is in the project (OD-39), but at 388 × 218 px it cannot serve large hero, print or high-density uses. No real photography yet. BR-MED rules prevent non-genuine imagery. |
| R-4 | 3D capture cost and device performance | **Deferred to Phase 7** | Budgets (NFR-PERF-4) and the fallback (BR-3D-5) are defined |
| R-5 | Scope breadth versus timeline | **Resolved** (OD-25: release slicing approved) | R1 scope as in the master plan |
| R-6 | Regulatory: DPDP, SMS DLT, WhatsApp templates | **Deferred to Phase 6/8/10** | Legal review (D-15). Provider registration should start early. |
| R-7 | Double booking / concurrency bugs | **Deferred to Phase 8** (implementation); design-level mitigation **in place** | MySQL harness proves lock-first serialisation and catches double booking (9/9 passing, 3 runs, MySQL 8.4.11). Booking-domain tests T-BK-01…10 must pass before booking code merges. |
| R-8 | Dev/prod parity (SQLite hid locking bugs) | **Resolved** — owner action A-1 pending | Tests forced to MySQL. A `*_testing` guard is verified to refuse the dev DB. |
| R-9 | Content inconsistency (prices, counts) | **Deferred to Phase 4/5** | Price tokens and inventory-driven blocks designed |
| R-10 | Lead spam and bots | **Deferred to Phase 8** | Form contract §3 in `../architecture/API_CONTRACTS.md` |
| R-11 | The only copy of the repository is on one machine | **Deferred to Phase 3 step 3.1** (OD-34) | Push to the private remote as the first Phase 3 step. Until then, keep a local backup of the project folder. **Update 2026-10-26:** an interim private personal backup exists on GitHub (OD-80); the company-organisation remote is still deferred (OD-79). |
| R-12 | **New:** slow client inputs keep Phase 1 open and delay Phase 2 | **Requires client input** | `../requirements/CLIENT_INPUTS_REQUIRED.md` is ready to send. Owner-approved deferrals are possible for non-structural items. |
| R-13 | **New:** "snapshot before lock" pitfall: a plain read before taking the lock causes double booking even with a lock | **Resolved at design level** | Reproduced by a negative-control test. ADR-0004 now requires the lock to be the first statement in the transaction. |
| R-14 | **New (2026-10-24):** the local `.env` connects as MySQL **`root`** on a server that also hosts other projects' databases, including personal-data tables such as candidates and employees. A misconfiguration or bug in SmartGro could read or damage another project's data. | **Requires owner approval** | Mitigations already in place: tests are forced to `smartgro_testing` and the `*_testing` guard blocks other databases. **Recommended:** switch to a least-privileged `smartgro` user limited to `smart_go` and `smartgro_testing` by running `SMARTGRO_DB_NAME=smart_go bash docs/operations/scripts/setup-local-mysql.sh`. Never use `root` in staging or production. |
| R-15 | Floors per property and full addresses of the four properties are unknown, so the ERD cannot be validated against real data | **Requires client input** (Q-OW-1 re-asked) | Grouping resolved (OD-13). Nothing is created by assumption (BR-PROP-1). Template SG-INV-1.3 is ready. |
| R-16 | **New:** the existing WordPress site at smartgro.space shows signs of compromise: 1,038 injected `/pin/` spam URLs, cloaked to serve Japanese spam to Googlebot | **Requires owner action now** (outside this project) | Evidence and recommended actions in `../research/EXISTING_WEBSITE_AUDIT.md` §2. The new platform must not reuse any files from it, and must return 410 for `/pin/*`. |
| R-17 | Loss of search visibility at cut-over from WordPress | **Deferred to Phase 11** (redirects approved, OD-21) | 57-URL inventory captured; final map from a fresh crawl and Search Console data; `/pin/*` → 410 |
| R-18 | Selling long-term agreements beyond a leased property's lease end | **Resolved for now** (OD-14: all current properties owned) | Lease tracking (BR-PROP-5) is activated when the first leased property is added |
| R-19 | Configuration breadth (7 categories × per-property rules) increases build and test effort | **Resolved** (OD-25, OD-26) | Typed configuration (ADR-0013 accepted); staged release |
| R-21 | **New (2026-10-25):** the D-31 destination conflicts with the decided organisation. GitHub has no organisation `Smartgro`, and the name `smartgro` belongs to a personal user account created 2017-11-03. A push to `Smartgro/smartgro` would place the code under a personal account (contrary to OD-29, OD-76 and OD-78). | **Requires owner action** | Owner to confirm the organisation's exact GitHub name, or whether that account is theirs and how ownership moves to an organisation. No remote, push or history change until resolved (register §0.24). Deferred with D-31 until local completion (OD-79). |
| R-20 | **New (2026-10-25):** with few staff, rules that need a second person block work. Examples: one person holds both the Super Admin and Finance roles, so legal details can never be verified; or only one person can approve content. | **Response approved** (owner, 2026-10-25): keep strict separation of duties. Staffing is still undecided (OD-57). | The rules are never relaxed, and the Super Admin cannot override them (SECURITY_MODEL §3.1, §11.2). SECURITY_MODEL §11.5 lists when a second authorised person is required: content, contact details, redirects, brand settings, tracking IDs, legal and GST verification, prices, imports, campaigns, and booking requests or reschedules entered by staff (OD-72). Until one exists, those actions wait; for example, invoices and legal pages stay blocked. **Recommended:** before launch, at least two people explicitly granted the permissions for each of those areas. |

| A-13 | **New (2026-10-28, OD-119 f):** NFR-PERF-4 ("viewer interactive ≤ 5 s on a mid-range phone over 4G") and the 15 MB limit cannot both hold on a slow 4G connection: at Lighthouse's slow-4G speed (1.47 Mbit/s) a model just under 15 MB took 83 s to download, and 5 s leaves well under 1 MB once the viewer has loaded. Decide before the public viewer is built (it needs published scenes, O-2): which 4G connection NFR-PERF-4 means; and whether the public viewer needs a smaller budget per floor, a lighter first view loaded before the full model, or a different target. Suggested: measure the pilot scene (D-13) on a real mid-range phone and network first; keep 15 MB as the upload limit meanwhile; serve the viewer module compressed in production (about 193 kB instead of 725 kB). Not a decision until you make it. **Update (2026-10-28, OD-121 e):** engineering cut the time to a usable viewer for small models by 11 to 37% on the emulated phone; for large models the time hardly changes, because the download is the time (88 s at the limits on slow 4G). The questions are unchanged and remain yours: which 4G NFR-PERF-4 means, and whether the public viewer needs a smaller budget per floor or a lighter first view. Production must compress JavaScript and WebAssembly (runbook). | `../requirements/PRD.md` NFR-PERF-4; `../testing/evidence/phase7/2026-10-28-od119/performance.json` |
